Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The particle_physics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 639 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 505 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_639(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 639. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^505(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)
